\r\n

Ellis Medicine is seeking an experienced Advanced Practitioner to join our Cardiology team in Glenville, New York.

\r\n\r\n

As a leading community healthcare system in New York's Capital Region, Ellis Medicine is dedicated to providing exceptional, compassionate, and patient-centered care. Our mission is to improve the health of the communities we serve through clinical excellence, innovation, education, and collaboration.

\r\n\r\n

This is an excellent opportunity to join a growing cardiology program and work alongside experienced cardiologists and an interdisciplinary team to provide high-quality cardiovascular care in an outpatient setting.

\r\n\r\n

Position Highlights

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Full-time outpatient cardiology position
    • \r\n\t
  • Join an established and collaborative cardiology team
    • \r\n\t
  • Work alongside experienced cardiologists and other advanced practice providers
    • \r\n\t
  • Opportunity to evaluate, diagnose, and manage patients with a broad range of cardiovascular conditions
    • \r\n\t
  • Strong focus on patient-centered, evidence-based care
    • \r\n\t
  • Opportunity to develop long-term relationships with patients
    • \r\n\t
  • Access to Ellis Medicine's comprehensive cardiac, specialty, diagnostic, and hospital services
    • \r\n\t
  • Support from the broader Ellis Medicine health system and specialty network
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Clinical Responsibilities

\r\n\r\n

The Advanced Practitioner will work collaboratively with cardiologists and the broader care team to provide comprehensive cardiovascular care.

\r\n\r\n

Responsibilities may include:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Evaluating new and established cardiology patients
    • \r\n\t
  • Obtaining comprehensive medical histories and performing physical examinations
    • \r\n\t
  • Assessing, diagnosing, and managing cardiovascular conditions
    • \r\n\t
  • Developing and implementing individualized treatment plans
    • \r\n\t
  • Ordering and interpreting appropriate diagnostic testing
    • \r\n\t
  • Managing medications and providing patient education
    • \r\n\t
  • Monitoring patients with chronic cardiovascular conditions
    • \r\n\t
  • Coordinating care with cardiologists, primary care providers, and other specialists
    • \r\n\t
  • Providing education regarding cardiovascular disease prevention, risk-factor modification, and healthy lifestyle choices
    • \r\n\t
  • Participating in care coordination and follow-up to promote optimal clinical outcomes
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Qualifications

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Current NP or PA certification
    • \r\n\t
  • Licensed (or eligible) to practice in New York State
    • \r\n\t
  • Previous experience as a Nurse Practitioner or Physician Assistant required
    • \r\n\t
  • Cardiology experience preferred
    • \r\n\t
  • Strong clinical judgment and communication skills
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to work collaboratively within a multidisciplinary team
    • \r\n\t

Why Ellis Medicine?

\r\n\r\n

The Wright Heart Center at Ellis Medicine is home to a comprehensive cardiac program serving patients throughout Schenectady County and the greater Capital Region. Ellis continues to invest in advanced diagnostic and treatment capabilities, innovative technologies, minimally invasive procedures, and coordinated cardiac care.

\r\n\r\n

Our integrated approach brings together cardiologists, advanced practice providers, nurses, diagnostic specialists, surgeons, and other healthcare professionals to provide patients with high-quality cardiovascular care close to home.

\r\n\r\n

Joining Ellis Medicine provides the opportunity to practice in a community-based healthcare system while having access to the resources and specialty services of a 438-bed teaching hospital.

\r\n\r\n

Join Our Team

\r\n\r\n

Join Ellis Medicine and become part of a dedicated cardiology team committed to providing exceptional cardiovascular care and improving the health and well-being of the communities we serve.

\r\n\r\n

Why Ellis Medicine?

\r\n\r\n

The Wright Heart Center at Ellis Medicine is home to a comprehensive cardiac program serving patients throughout Schenectady County and the greater Capital Region. Ellis continues to invest in advanced diagnostic and treatment capabilities, innovative technologies, minimally invasive procedures, and coordinated cardiac care.

\r\n\r\n

Our integrated approach brings together cardiologists, advanced practice providers, nurses, diagnostic specialists, surgeons, and other healthcare professionals to provide patients with high-quality cardiovascular care close to home.

\r\n\r\n

Joining Ellis Medicine provides the opportunity to practice in a community-based healthcare system while having access to the resources and specialty services of a 438-bed teaching hospital.

\r\n\r\n

Join Our Team

\r\n\r\n

Join Ellis Medicine and become part of a dedicated cardiology team committed to providing exceptional cardiovascular care and improving the health and well-being of the communities we serve.

\r\n\r\n

Ellis Medicine is seeking a Per Diem Hospital Medicine Advanced Practitioner to join our collaborative Hospital Medicine team. This is an excellent opportunity for an experienced APP or new graduate seeking flexible clinical work within a well-supported Hospital Medicine program.

\r\n\r\n

Our APPs play an integral role in the care of hospitalized patients, working collaboratively with physicians while managing a dedicated patient census. With strong physician support, broad specialty availability, a closed ICU and no required procedures, our APPs can focus on providing high-quality, patient-centered care.

\r\n\r\n

Ellis Medicine is seeking a Primary Care Advanced Practitioner to join our primary care practice in Glenville, NY.

\r\n\r\n

This is an excellent opportunity for a Nurse Practitioner or Physician Assistant who enjoys providing comprehensive care to patients across the lifespan and wants to become part of a collaborative, community-focused primary care practice.

\r\n\r\n

Our Glenville practice serves approximately 12,000 patients and cares for both children and adults. The practice offers an established patient population, dedicated clinical support, and an opportunity for an APP to develop meaningful, long-term relationships with patients and families.

\r\n\r\n

    • \r\n\t

Ellis Medicine is seeking a full-time Hospitalist APP to join our collaborative and supportive inpatient team. This is an excellent opportunity for an experienced APP or new graduate looking to practice alongside hospitalists and a multidisciplinary care team in a well-supported hospital medicine environment.

\r\n\r\n

Our APPs play an integral role in the care of hospitalized patients, working collaboratively with physicians while managing a dedicated patient census. The practice offers a predictable schedule, strong physician support, broad specialty resources and no required procedures.

\r\n\r\n

\r\n\r\n

    • \r\n\t
  • Coordination of care with physicians, nursing, consultants, patients and families
    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t
  • Participation in multidisciplinary care planning
    • \r\n\t
  • Appropriate consultation and coordination with specialty services

  • Providing high-quality, evidence-based inpatient care

Ellis Medicine is seeking a Primary Care Advanced Practitioner to join our McClellan Street primary care practice in Schenectady, NY.

\r\n\r\n

This is an excellent opportunity for a Nurse Practitioner or Physician Assistant who enjoys providing comprehensive care to patients across the lifespan and wants to become part of a collaborative, community-focused primary care practice.

\r\n\r\n

Our Schenectady practice serves a diverse patient population that includes both children and adults.  The practice offers an established patient population, dedicated clinical support, and an opportunity for an APP to develop meaningful, long-term relationships with patients and families.

\r\n
Basic Function:
\r\nThe Nurse Manager (NM) actualizes the vision, mission and values for Ellis Medicine and maintains standards of the practice for nursing as defined by the American Nurses Association, Code of Ethics, Nursing Scope and Standards of Practice and Nursing’s social Policy Statement. The Nurse Manager participates in performance improvement/research activities; maintains professional practice standards and clinical expertise; and demonstrates leadership skills. The Nurse Manager demonstrates core and unit specific competencies based on the patient population served. This position is accountable for maintaining all clinical administrative functions (patient care delivery, fiscal, material and human resources) of nursing unit(s) on a 24-hour basis. The position is responsible to create an environment in which autonomy is nurtured, encouraged and reflected in a highly competent workforce capable of providing quality care. Professional interdisciplinary collaboration, communication and teamwork enacted enable care delivery to the customers and community to improve health and wellness. The position is responsible to uphold compliance with Ellis Medicine’s policies/procedures, service excellence standards and regulatory requirements.
\r\n 
\r\nRequirements:
\r\nCurrently licensed in New York State as a Registered Nurse and a graduate of an approved school of nursing. A Bachelor of Science in Nursing is required, MSN preferred or in progress. Three (3)- five (5) years' acute care experience preferred. Excellent interpersonal relationships, effective communication skills, (written, oral and interpersonal) ability to problem solve, ability to motivate, develop and coach individuals and groups, ability to appropriately confront issues, and ability to think creatively. A strong commitment to furthering institutional performance measures through interdisciplinary teamwork is essential.
\r\n \r\n

GUIDING BEHAVIORS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Communication:  Listen to understand, before seeking to be understood.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaboration:  Deliver outstanding service ensuring that safety comes first.
    • \r\n\t
  • Compassion:  Serve every person with empathy, dignity, and compassion.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

PHYSICAL REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Should be able to push/pull, lift/move 35 lb., be able to perform moderately difficult manual manipulations such as using a keyboard, writing, and filing for extended periods of time, must be able to perform tasks which require hand-eye coordination such as data entry, typing and using photo copiers.  Mobility requirements may include the ability to be stationary at a workstation for a prolonged period time in addition to being able to squat or be mobile for a reasonable length of time and distance.  Communication requirements include the ability to comprehend the spoken English language in addition to being able to communicate and read the English language.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Responsibilities:

\r\n  • The Nurse Manager will work collaboratively with the DON to plan, implement, and monitor the nursing care delivery system to assure effective and efficient utilization of staff and other resources for the delivery of patient care.
\r\n  • Creates/validates daily assignments that reflect appropriate assignments for all staff and their responsibility for patient care based on skill levels and patient care needs.
\r\n  • Assures that an adequate number of nursing personnel are scheduled and available to meet patient care needs which include proactively analyzing resources and personnel needs.
\r\n  • Discuss with staff the effectiveness of systems in place with focus on continuous improvement and the continuum of care.
\r\n  • Facilitates and promotes the delivery of patient/family centered care to a diverse customer population by upholding the standards of excellent patient care always.
\r\n  • Manages unscheduled absences and tardiness according to policy.
\r\n  • Collaborates with Human Resource business partner regarding staff discipline according to policy.
\r\n  • Assesses and reassigns staff members in to effectively and efficiently meet the needs of the unit and organization.
\r\n  • Collaborates with the DON to create an environment for staff participation in performance improvement activities that assures the care of the particular patient population meets or exceeds institutional and regulatory standards.

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

\r\n\t\t\t
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 SECTION I

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION: 

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Education:  Graduate of accredited RN program required. Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for limited permit to practice is required.  Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred. Current BLS is required upon hire, ACLS and NRP must be obtained within 6 months.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Experience:  Prior OB and/or acute care experience preferred.  Basic computer skills and experience with computerized charting.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Certification:  Board Certification preferred

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

MAKING ELLIS EXCEPTIONAL (MEE) BEHAVIORS & STANDARDS

\r\n\r\n

                       

\r\n\r\n

SECTION V:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team and including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses patient teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implements and /or coordinates the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.
    • \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

\r\nThe Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.
\r\n 
\r\nRequirements:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of accredited RN program required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for limited permit to practice is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred; candidates without a BSN will be required to start a program within 2 years of hire and complete by year 6 of employment.
    • \r\n\t
  • A minimum of 1 year of acute care experience preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current BLS is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current certification in ACLS, PALS and NRP may be required depending on unit scope.
    • \r\n\t
  • Board Certification preferred.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Responsibilities:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team and including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses patient teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implements and /or coordinates the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.
    • \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                        CT Technologist

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Radiology

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Supervisor of CT

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The essential task of a CT Technologist is operating computed tomography equipment and accessories. Able to perform tube warm-ups and calibrations daily. CT Technologists apply principles of radiation protection during exams to minimize unnecessary exposure.  Perform all CT examinations, CT biopsy’s, 3D renderings and reformations, etc. A vast knowledge and understanding of cross-sectional anatomy, vascular anatomy and abnormal pathology is essential. 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of an accredited Radiologic Technologist Education program
    • \r\n\t
  • Current NYS license in Radiologic Technology required, with injection certification.
    • \r\n\t
  • ARRT Certification required, Computed Tomography preferred at hire or obtained within 24 months of hire.
    • \r\n\t
  • 2 or more years of experience as CT Technologist is preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current Basic Life Support (CPR) certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

GUIDING BEHAVIORS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Communication:  Listen to understand, before seeking to be understood.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaboration:  Deliver outstanding service ensuring that safety comes first.
    • \r\n\t
  • Compassion:  Serve every person with empathy, dignity, and compassion.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

\r\n\t\t\t
\r\n\r\n

SECTION IV:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PHYSICAL REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Should be able to push/pull, lift/move 35 lb., be able to perform moderately difficult manual manipulations such as using a keyboard, writing, and filing for extended periods of time, must be able to perform tasks which require hand-eye coordination such as data entry, typing and using photo copiers.  Mobility requirements may include the ability to be stationary at a workstation for a prolonged period time in addition to being able to squat or be mobile for a reasonable length of time and distance.  Communication requirements include the ability to comprehend the spoken English language in addition to being able to communicate and read the English language.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION V

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Perform all CT exams under the direction of a Radiologist.
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to work in a team environment with a positive attitude.
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to assess lab values that pertain to exam.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operates specialized imaging equipment to produce clinical diagnostic images, as directed by physicians.
    • \r\n\t
  • Performs CT scan studies and Special Procedures of anatomical areas as ordered/directed, according to established standards and procedures.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operate all radiographic imaging equipment associated with Special Procedures, Computed Tomography, including but not limited to monitors, injectors, scanners.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare IV Contrast Power injectors.
    • \r\n\t
  • Maintains a satisfactory attendance record.
    • \r\n\t
  • On-call and holiday rotation assignments required as assigned
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Administers Contrast Media as required.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Knowledge of cross-sectional anatomy, positioning and imaging techniques.
    • \r\n\t
  • Properly and accurately screen patients for CT (Lab work to identify appropriate lab values, identify allergy contraindications, and confirming diagnosis and patient complaint match)
    • \r\n\t
  • Track, scan, verify studies and complete all required paperwork associated with such.
    • \r\n\t
  • Monitor PACS for Problem Studies and Call Reports as measure of default when dedicated person unable.
    • \r\n\t
  • Document on requisition appropriate needed information, obtain and verifies patients’ clinical history, ensuring that information is documented and readily available to the licensed practitioner.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepares patients for procedures, providing instructions to obtain desired results, gain cooperation, and minimize anxiety.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates images and data for technical quality, manipulates display parameters assuring proper identification is evident.
    • \r\n\t
  • Selects proper technical factors and operates equipment and accessories as directed. Positions patients and equipment to best demonstrate the anatomic area of interest, respecting patient ability and comfort.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare solutions for patients to drink. Barium, and other contrast agents.
    • \r\n\t
  • Utilize shielding as necessary for patient and staff.
    • \r\n\t
  • Rotate shifts and campus as departmental needs require.
    • \r\n\t
  • Attention to detail.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Adheres to the policies and procedures outlined in the Exposure Control Manual regarding; universal precautions, bloodborne pathogen exposure, disposal of needles, syringes, and sharps, clean up procedures for blood and body fluid spills, storage, handling and disposal of medical waste.
    • \r\n\t
  • The staff member demonstrates the knowledge and skill necessary to provide care, based on physical, psychosocial, educational and safety related criteria, appropriate to the age of the patients served in his/her assigned service area. The skills and knowledge needed to provide such care may be gained through education, training and/or experience.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to hospital policies and procedures related to; mandatory education, annual health assessment, licensure verification, DOH and The Joint Commission
    • \r\n

Essential Job Responsibilities

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t
  • Operate and maintain equipment, reporting any malfunctions promptly.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritize and coordinate imaging procedures based on clinical urgency.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborate with radiologists, nursing staff, and emergency/critical care teams.
    • \r\n\t
  • Follow ALARA principles and adhere to radiation safety rules.
    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t
  • Operates specialized imaging equipment to produce clinical diagnostic images, as directed by physicians
    • \r\n\t
  • Performs CT scan studies and Special Procedures of anatomical areas as ordered/directed, according to established standards and procedures
    • \r\n\t
  • Operate all radiographic imaging equipment associated with Special Procedures, Computed Tomography, including but not limited to monitors, injectors, scanners
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare IV Contrast Power injectors
    • \r\n\t
  • Maintains a satisfactory attendance record
    • \r\n\t
  • Administers Contrast Media as required
    • \r\n\t
  • Knowledge of cross-sectional anatomy, positioning and imaging techniques.
    • \r\n\t
  • Properly and accurately screen patients for CT (Lab work to identify appropriate lab values, identify allergy contraindications, and confirm the diagnosis and patient complaint match)
    • \r\n\t
  • Track, scan, verify studies and complete all required paperwork associated with such. Monitor PACS for Problem Studies and Call Reports as a measure of default when a dedicated person is unable.
    • \r\n\t
  • Document on requisition appropriate needed information, obtains and verifies patients’ clinical history, ensuring that information is documented and readily available to the licensed practitioner.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepares patients for procedures, providing instructions to obtain desired results, gain cooperation, and minimize anxiety.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates images and data for technical quality, manipulates display parameters, ensuring proper identification is evident.
    • \r\n\t
  • Selects proper technical factors and operates equipment and accessories as directed. Positions patients and equipment to best demonstrate the anatomic area of interest, respecting patient ability and comfort.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare solutions for patients to drink. Barium, and other contrast agents.
    • \r\n\t
  • Utilize shielding as necessary for patient and staff.
    • \r\n

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                        CT Technologist

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Radiology

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Supervisor of CT

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The essential task of a CT Technologist is operating computed tomography equipment and accessories. Able to perform tube warm-ups and calibrations daily. CT Technologists apply principles of radiation protection during exams to minimize unnecessary exposure.  Perform all CT examinations, CT biopsy’s, 3D renderings and reformations, etc. A vast knowledge and understanding of cross-sectional anatomy, vascular anatomy and abnormal pathology is essential. 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of an accredited Radiologic Technologist Education program
    • \r\n\t
  • Current NYS license in Radiologic Technology required, with injection certification.
    • \r\n\t
  • ARRT Certification required, Computed Tomography preferred at hire or obtained within 24 months of hire.
    • \r\n\t
  • 2 or more years of experience as CT Technologist is preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current Basic Life Support (CPR) certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of an accredited Radiologic Technologist Education program.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current NYS license in Radiologic Technology required, with injection certification.
    • \r\n\t
  • ARRT Certification required, Computed Tomography preferred at hire or obtained within 1 year of hire. 2 or more years of experience as CT Technologist is preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current Basic Life Support (CPR) certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

PHYSICAL REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Should be able to push/pull, lift/move 15 lb., be able to perform moderately difficult manual manipulations such as using a keyboard, writing, and filing for extended periods of time, must be able to perform tasks which require hand-eye coordination such as data entry, typing and using photo copiers.  Mobility requirements may include the ability to be stationary at a workstation for a prolonged period time in addition to being able to squat or be mobile for a reasonable length of time and distance.  Communication requirements include the ability to comprehend the spoken English language in addition to being able to communicate and read the English language.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                        CT Technologist

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Radiology

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Supervisor of CT

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The essential task of a CT Technologist is operating computed tomography equipment and accessories. Able to perform tube warm-ups and calibrations daily. CT Technologists apply principles of radiation protection during exams to minimize unnecessary exposure.  Perform all CT examinations, CT biopsy’s, 3D renderings and reformations, etc. A vast knowledge and understanding of cross-sectional anatomy, vascular anatomy and abnormal pathology is essential. 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of an accredited Radiologic Technologist Education program
    • \r\n\t
  • Current NYS license in Radiologic Technology required, with injection certification.
    • \r\n\t
  • ARRT Certification required, Computed Tomography preferred at hire or obtained within 24 months of hire.
    • \r\n\t
  • 2 or more years of experience as CT Technologist is preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current Basic Life Support (CPR) certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

GUIDING BEHAVIORS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Communication:  Listen to understand, before seeking to be understood.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaboration:  Deliver outstanding service ensuring that safety comes first.
    • \r\n\t
  • Compassion:  Serve every person with empathy, dignity, and compassion.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n\r\n\t\r\n\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\r\n\t\r\n
\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t
\r\n\r\n

SECTION IV:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PHYSICAL REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Should be able to push/pull, lift/move 35 lb., be able to perform moderately difficult manual manipulations such as using a keyboard, writing, and filing for extended periods of time, must be able to perform tasks which require hand-eye coordination such as data entry, typing and using photo copiers.  Mobility requirements may include the ability to be stationary at a workstation for a prolonged period time in addition to being able to squat or be mobile for a reasonable length of time and distance.  Communication requirements include the ability to comprehend the spoken English language in addition to being able to communicate and read the English language.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION V

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Perform all CT exams under the direction of a Radiologist.
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to work in a team environment with a positive attitude.
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to assess lab values that pertain to exam.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operates specialized imaging equipment to produce clinical diagnostic images, as directed by physicians.
    • \r\n\t
  • Performs CT scan studies and Special Procedures of anatomical areas as ordered/directed, according to established standards and procedures.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operate all radiographic imaging equipment associated with Special Procedures, Computed Tomography, including but not limited to monitors, injectors, scanners.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare IV Contrast Power injectors.
    • \r\n\t
  • Maintains a satisfactory attendance record.
    • \r\n\t
  • On-call and holiday rotation assignments required as assigned
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Administers Contrast Media as required.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Knowledge of cross-sectional anatomy, positioning and imaging techniques.
    • \r\n\t
  • Properly and accurately screen patients for CT (Lab work to identify appropriate lab values, identify allergy contraindications, and confirming diagnosis and patient complaint match)
    • \r\n\t
  • Track, scan, verify studies and complete all required paperwork associated with such.
    • \r\n\t
  • Monitor PACS for Problem Studies and Call Reports as measure of default when dedicated person unable.
    • \r\n\t
  • Document on requisition appropriate needed information, obtain and verifies patients’ clinical history, ensuring that information is documented and readily available to the licensed practitioner.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepares patients for procedures, providing instructions to obtain desired results, gain cooperation, and minimize anxiety.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates images and data for technical quality, manipulates display parameters assuring proper identification is evident.
    • \r\n\t
  • Selects proper technical factors and operates equipment and accessories as directed. Positions patients and equipment to best demonstrate the anatomic area of interest, respecting patient ability and comfort.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare solutions for patients to drink. Barium, and other contrast agents.
    • \r\n\t
  • Utilize shielding as necessary for patient and staff.
    • \r\n\t
  • Rotate shifts and campus as departmental needs require.
    • \r\n\t
  • Attention to detail.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Adheres to the policies and procedures outlined in the Exposure Control Manual regarding; universal precautions, bloodborne pathogen exposure, disposal of needles, syringes, and sharps, clean up procedures for blood and body fluid spills, storage, handling and disposal of medical waste.
    • \r\n\t
  • The staff member demonstrates the knowledge and skill necessary to provide care, based on physical, psychosocial, educational and safety related criteria, appropriate to the age of the patients served in his/her assigned service area. The skills and knowledge needed to provide such care may be gained through education, training and/or experience.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to hospital policies and procedures related to; mandatory education, annual health assessment, licensure verification, DOH and The Joint Commission
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

  • ARRT Certification required, Computed Tomography preferred at hire or obtained within 24 months of hire.
    • \r\n\t
  • 2 or more years of experience as CT Technologist is preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current Basic Life Support (CPR) certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

GUIDING BEHAVIORS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Communication:  Listen to understand, before seeking to be understood.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaboration:  Deliver outstanding service ensuring that safety comes first.
    • \r\n\t
  • Compassion:  Serve every person with empathy, dignity, and compassion.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n\r\n\t\r\n\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\t\r\n\t\t\r\n\t\r\n
\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t		\r\n\t\t\t

 

\r\n\t\t\t
\r\n\r\n

SECTION IV:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PHYSICAL REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Should be able to push/pull, lift/move 35 lb., be able to perform moderately difficult manual manipulations such as using a keyboard, writing, and filing for extended periods of time, must be able to perform tasks which require hand-eye coordination such as data entry, typing and using photo copiers.  Mobility requirements may include the ability to be stationary at a workstation for a prolonged period time in addition to being able to squat or be mobile for a reasonable length of time and distance.  Communication requirements include the ability to comprehend the spoken English language in addition to being able to communicate and read the English language.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION V

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Perform all CT exams under the direction of a Radiologist.
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to work in a team environment with a positive attitude.
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to assess lab values that pertain to exam.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operates specialized imaging equipment to produce clinical diagnostic images, as directed by physicians.
    • \r\n\t
  • Performs CT scan studies and Special Procedures of anatomical areas as ordered/directed, according to established standards and procedures.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operate all radiographic imaging equipment associated with Special Procedures, Computed Tomography, including but not limited to monitors, injectors, scanners.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare IV Contrast Power injectors.
    • \r\n\t
  • Maintains a satisfactory attendance record.
    • \r\n\t
  • On-call and holiday rotation assignments required as assigned
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Administers Contrast Media as required.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Knowledge of cross-sectional anatomy, positioning and imaging techniques.
    • \r\n\t
  • Properly and accurately screen patients for CT (Lab work to identify appropriate lab values, identify allergy contraindications, and confirming diagnosis and patient complaint match)
    • \r\n\t
  • Track, scan, verify studies and complete all required paperwork associated with such.
    • \r\n\t
  • Monitor PACS for Problem Studies and Call Reports as measure of default when dedicated person unable.
    • \r\n\t
  • Document on requisition appropriate needed information, obtain and verifies patients’ clinical history, ensuring that information is documented and readily available to the licensed practitioner.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepares patients for procedures, providing instructions to obtain desired results, gain cooperation, and minimize anxiety.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates images and data for technical quality, manipulates display parameters assuring proper identification is evident.
    • \r\n\t
  • Selects proper technical factors and operates equipment and accessories as directed. Positions patients and equipment to best demonstrate the anatomic area of interest, respecting patient ability and comfort.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare solutions for patients to drink. Barium, and other contrast agents.
    • \r\n\t
  • Utilize shielding as necessary for patient and staff.
    • \r\n\t
  • Rotate shifts and campus as departmental needs require.
    • \r\n\t
  • Attention to detail.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Adheres to the policies and procedures outlined in the Exposure Control Manual regarding; universal precautions, bloodborne pathogen exposure, disposal of needles, syringes, and sharps, clean up procedures for blood and body fluid spills, storage, handling and disposal of medical waste.
    • \r\n\t
  • The staff member demonstrates the knowledge and skill necessary to provide care, based on physical, psychosocial, educational and safety related criteria, appropriate to the age of the patients served in his/her assigned service area. The skills and knowledge needed to provide such care may be gained through education, training and/or experience.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to hospital policies and procedures related to; mandatory education, annual health assessment, licensure verification, DOH and The Joint Commission
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                        CT Technologist

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Radiology

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Supervisor of CT

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The essential task of a CT Technologist is operating computed tomography equipment and accessories. Able to perform tube warm-ups and calibrations daily. CT Technologists apply principles of radiation protection during exams to minimize unnecessary exposure.  Perform all CT examinations, CT biopsy’s, 3D renderings and reformations, etc. A vast knowledge and understanding of cross-sectional anatomy, vascular anatomy and abnormal pathology is essential. 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENTS:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of an accredited Radiologic Technologist Education program
    • \r\n\t
  • Current NYS license in Radiologic Technology required, with injection certification.
    • \r\n\t
  • ARRT Certification required, Computed Tomography preferred at hire or obtained within 24 months of hire.
    • \r\n\t
  • 2 or more years of experience as CT Technologist is preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current Basic Life Support (CPR) certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

GUIDING BEHAVIORS:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Communication:  Listen to understand, before seeking to be understood.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaboration:  Deliver outstanding service ensuring that safety comes first.
    • \r\n\t
  • Compassion:  Serve every person with empathy, dignity, and compassion.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Exceptional Pay & Generous DAY ONE Benefits and Shift Differentials!

10 AM-10PM

36 Hours

Do you love to take care of people?  Are you a team player that thrives on a diverse team of professionals?  At Ellis Medicine, we believe that communication, collaboration and compassion are essential to realizing our mission to meet the health and wellness needs of our community with excellence. We are looking for a candidate for the CT Technologist position that shares these values.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

What will you do as an CT Technologist?

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Perform CT exams, biopsies, 3D renderings, and special procedures under the radiologist's direction.
    • \r\n\t
  • Operate CT and radiographic equipment, including injectors and scanners
    • \r\n\t
  • Administer IV and oral contrast (with proper certification)
    • \r\n\t
  • Screen patients, assess lab values, and review clinical history.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prepare patients and explain procedures to ensure comfort and cooperation
    • \r\n\t
  • Monitor PACS, verify studies, and complete necessary documentation
    • \r\n\t
  • Maintain infection control and radiation safety standards
    • \r\n\t
  • Rotate shifts, including on-call and holiday assignments as needed
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

What do you need to be an CT Technologist?

\r\n\r\n

Education and Experience Requirements

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of an accredited Radiologic Technology program
    • \r\n\t
  • NYS Radiologic Technologist license with injection certification
    • \r\n\t
  • ARRT certification (CT preferred or obtained within 1 year)
    • \r\n\t
  • 2+ years of CT experience preferred
    • \r\n\t
  • BLS (CPR) certification required
    • \r\n

    \r\n\t
  • Must be able to lift up to 15 lbs.,
    • \r\n\t
  • Perform repetitive tasks such as data entry
    • \r\n\t
  • Remain mobile and stationary as needed for long periods.
    • \r\n

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                         Registered Nurse

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Nursing / Patient Care Services

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Nurse Manager

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENT(S):

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of accredited RN program required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for limited permit to practice is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred and candidates without a BSN will be required to complete the degree depending on previous experience and specific position applying for.
    • \r\n\t
  • Acute care experience preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current BLS is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current certification in ACLS, PALS and NRP may be required depending on unit scope. Board Certification preferred.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

MAKING ELLIS EXCEPTIONAL (MEE) BEHAVIORS & STANDARDS

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION IV:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team and including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses patient teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implements and /or coordinates the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon patient condition and age.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                         Registered Nurse

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Nursing / Patient Care Services

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Nurse Manager

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENT(S):

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of accredited RN program required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for limited permit to practice is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred and candidates without a BSN will be required to complete the degree depending on previous experience and specific position applying for.
    • \r\n\t
  • Acute care experience preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current BLS is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current certification in ACLS, PALS and NRP may be required depending on unit scope. Board Certification preferred.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

MAKING ELLIS EXCEPTIONAL (MEE) BEHAVIORS & STANDARDS

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION IV:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team and including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses patient teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implements and /or coordinates the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon patient condition and age.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION V:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

\r\n

Ellis Medicine has GENEROUS and FLEXIBLE benefits offerings!

\r\n\r\n

TOP PAY. TOP BENEFITS.

\r\n\r\n

And, support that values you.

\r\n\r\n

At Ellis, we don’t just hire nurses. We invest in them. And, with better staffing levels, you have time to focus on what really matters: patient care. Not only will you enjoy exceptional pay and benefits, but you’ll be able to thrive in a more supportive working environment.

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.
\r\n 

\r\n\r\n

Responsibilities:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement, utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including the patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team, including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses the patient's teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implement and /or coordinate the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon the patient's condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.
    • \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Requirements:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduation from an accredited RN program required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for a limited permit to practice is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred; candidates without a BSN will be required to start a program within 2 years of hire and complete by year 6 of employment.
    • \r\n\t
  • A minimum of 1 year of acute care experience preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • A current BLS is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current certification in ACLS, PALS and
    • \r\n\t
  • NRP may be required depending on unit scope.
    • \r\n\t
  • Board Certification preferred.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Ellis Medicine is committed to creating a diverse environment and is proud to be an equal opportunity employer. All qualified applicants will receive consideration for employment without regard to, and will prohibit, discrimination on the basis of race, creed, color, religion, sex/gender (including pregnancy), age, national origin, disability (including pregnancy-related conditions), genetic information, predisposition or carrier status, military or veteran status, prior arrest or conviction record, marital or familial status, sexual orientation, transgender status, gender identity, gender expression, reproductive health decisions, domestic violence victim status, known relationship or association with any member of a protected class, and any other characteristic protected by applicable law violates federal, state and, where applicable, local laws , reproductive health decisions or source of payment, consistent with applicable legislation and to comply with the laws pertaining thereto.

\r\n\r\n

Salary Range:  $41.00-$58.08/hour                  Pay is based on experience, skills, and education. Exempt positions under the Fair Labor Standards Act (FLSA) will be paid within the base salary equivalent of the stated hourly rates. The pay range may also vary within the stated range based on location.

TITLE:                         Registered Nurse

DEPARTMENT:         Nursing / Patient Care Services

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENT(S):

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of accredited RN program required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for limited permit to practice is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred and candidates without a BSN will be required to complete the degree depending on previous experience and specific position applying for.
    • \r\n\t
  • Acute care experience preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current BLS is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current certification in ACLS, PALS and NRP may be required depending on unit scope. Board Certification preferred.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.

ELLIS MEDICINE

\r\n\r\n

POSITION DESCRIPTION

\r\n\r\n
\r\n

 

\r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

TITLE:                         Registered Nurse

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

DEPARTMENT:         Nursing / Patient Care Services

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

REPORTS TO:           Nurse Manager

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION I:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

BASIC FUNCTION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION II:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE REQUIREMENT(S):

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Graduate of accredited RN program required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for limited permit to practice is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred and candidates without a BSN will be required to complete the degree depending on previous experience and specific position applying for.
    • \r\n\t
  • Acute care experience preferred.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current BLS is required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Current certification in ACLS, PALS and NRP may be required depending on unit scope. Board Certification preferred.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION III:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

MAKING ELLIS EXCEPTIONAL (MEE) BEHAVIORS & STANDARDS

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

SECTION IV:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

PRIMARY RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE POSITION:

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team and including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses patient teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implements and /or coordinates the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon patient condition and age.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations. # Responsibilities: Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources. Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses. Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode. Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age. Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions. Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members. Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement, utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including the patient/family. Communicates the patient#s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team, including the medical record. Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description. Assesses the patient#s teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient#s educational level, learning readiness, and age. Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implement and /or coordinate the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration. Provides a safe environment based upon the patient#s condition and age. Assumes responsibility for education and self- development. Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required. Adheres to institutional policies and procedures. Requirements: Graduation from an accredited RN program required. Current licensure in NYS or eligibility for a limited permit to practice is required. Bachelor of Science in Nursing is preferred; candidates without a BSN will be required to start a program within 2 years of hire and complete by year 6 of employment. A minimum of 1 year of acute care experience preferred. A current BLS is required. Current certification in ACLS, PALS and NRP may be required depending on unit scope. Board Certification preferred. The Registered Professional Nurse cares for patients by diagnosing and treating their responses to actual or potential health problems in the acute care setting through implementation of the nursing process. Coordinates care of other disciplines, delegates tasks to other nursing personnel, supervises care, provides direct patient care and evaluates outcomes. Maintains competence in clinical practice and assists in the development of nursing practice standards through participation in professional committees and organizations.
\r\n 

\r\n\r\n

Responsibilities:

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Completes patient assessment using subjective and objective data sources.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates assessment data and establishes nursing diagnoses.
    • \r\n\t
  • Prioritizes patient needs including physiological, social, psychological, spiritual, age specific and selects diagnoses which can be reasonably addressed in the acute care episode.
    • \r\n\t
  • Develops an individualized PCP based on patient assessment using nursing practice standards and protocols, realistic measurable outcomes/goal and timeframes for goal achievement according to patient condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Delivers patient care as outlined in the PCP and patient response to interventions.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assigns patient care tasks to and observes care given by non-RN staff members.
    • \r\n\t
  • Evaluates patient care and assesses the level and timeliness of outcome achievement, utilizing input from pertinent members of the healthcare team, including the patient/family.
    • \r\n\t
  • Communicates the patient’s plan of care to all members of the healthcare team, including the medical record.
    • \r\n\t
  • Organizes and communicates nursing care assignments/tasks/responsibilities based upon patient acuity/need and staff education/skill/job description.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assesses the patient's teaching needs as part of the initial and ongoing assessment, sets goals and develops teaching plans consistent with the patient’s educational level, learning readiness, and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Collaborates with the interdisciplinary team to implement and /or coordinate the teaching plan focusing on wellness, health maintenance and restoration.
    • \r\n\t
  • Provides a safe environment based upon the patient's condition and age.
    • \r\n\t
  • Assumes responsibility for education and self- development.
    • \r\n\t
  • Participates in nursing performance improvement and interdisciplinary research and study activities as required.
    • \r\n\t
  • Adheres to institutional policies and procedures.
    • \r\n
